# Agents · F · part 8

1,039 entries, fiscal-mei to flashcard-refiner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fiscal-mei**: Cuida do MEI: DAS-MEI de 2026, limite de receita (com a mudança da LC 238/2026 para 2027 e 2028), ocupações permitidas, DASN-SIMEI, excesso até e acima de 20%, desenquadramento e migração para ME, empregado, MEI caminhoneiro e nota fiscal. Use ao receber CCMEI, extrato do PGMEI ou relatório mensal de receitas, ou dúvida se o cliente pode ser ou continuar MEI. Não use para ME/EPP do Simples (fiscal-simples-nacional). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/fiscal-mei.md) · ★1)
- **fiscal-multiplier-specialist**: Estimates and simulates fiscal multipliers: state-dependent, composition-dependent, open-economy, DSGE and empirical evidence. ([tatashbar/Economic-Modeling-Studio](https://github.com/tatashbar/Economic-Modeling-Studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fiscal-multiplier-specialist.md) · ★4)
- **fiscal-pis-cofins**: Apura PIS e COFINS cumulativos e não cumulativos a fundo: débitos por CST, créditos das Leis 10.637 e 10.833 com insumos pelo critério do STJ, monofásico, alíquota zero, exclusão do ICMS e saldo credor. Use ao receber XMLs, razão de custos, EFD-Contribuições ou dúvida de crédito e insumo. Não use para a guia comum do Presumido (fiscal-lucro-presumido) nem para gerar a EFD (obrigacoes-efd-contribuicoes). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/fiscal-pis-cofins.md) · ★1)
- **fiscal-policy-specialist**: Domain expert in Romer & Romer methodology, fiscal shock identification, motivation categories, and macro evaluation. Consulted by strategy-reviewer for fiscal policy questions. ([estebandegetau/Fiscal-shocks](https://github.com/estebandegetau/Fiscal-shocks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fiscal-policy-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **fiscal-requisitante**: Fiscal Requisitante do Contrato de TIC (IN SGD/ME 94/2022, art. 2º, V, "d", e art. 33, III). Use na Gestão do Contrato para avaliar a execução do ponto de vista do negócio e da funcionalidade da solução - se a entrega resolve a necessidade, qualidade percebida pelos usuários, não conformidades funcionais e manutenção da necessidade, economicidade e oportunidade da contratação. Grava a avaliação para o Gestor do Contrato consolidar. ([marquesmarcelo/ia-contratacao-tic](https://github.com/marquesmarcelo/ia-contratacao-tic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fiscal-requisitante.md))
- **fiscal-retencoes-federais**: Calcula e confere as retenções de notas de serviço tomadas e prestadas: IRRF de 1,5% ou 1%, CSRF de 4,65%, INSS de 11% ou 3,5% em cessão de mão de obra e empreitada, ISS retido e retenção de órgão público federal (IN RFB 1.234/2012), com dispensas, Simples e reflexo na EFD-Reinf. Use ao receber NFS-e, contrato de terceirização ou pagamento a fornecedor PJ. Não use para transmitir a Reinf (obrigacoes-dctfweb-reinf). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/fiscal-retencoes-federais.md) · ★1)
- **fiscal-risk-specialist**: Assesses fiscal risks: contingent liabilities, SOEs, financial-sector support, disasters, pensions, climate and demographics. ([tatashbar/Economic-Modeling-Studio](https://github.com/tatashbar/Economic-Modeling-Studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fiscal-risk-specialist.md) · ★4)
- **fiscal-simples-nacional**: Calcula e confere o DAS do Simples Nacional como o PGDAS-D: anexo (I a V), RBT12, alíquota efetiva, fator R e segregação de ICMS-ST, monofásico, ISS retido, exportação e locação de bens móveis, com teste de sublimite e teto. Use ao receber faturamento, XMLs ou extrato do PGDAS-D de optante, ou pedido de DAS ou fator R. Não use para MEI (fiscal-mei) nem para escolher regime (consultoria-planejamento-tributario). ([adrianokelvio/agente-contabil](https://github.com/adrianokelvio/agente-contabil/blob/HEAD/agents/fiscal-simples-nacional.md) · ★1)
- **fiscal-specialist**: Use em qualquer trabalho de NFS-e, ISS, retenções ou obrigações acessórias. Conhece as seções 9.6 e 9.10.3 do prompt único. ([alceupassos/titan](https://github.com/alceupassos/titan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fiscal-specialist.md))
- **fiscal-tecnico**: Fiscal Técnico do Contrato de TIC (IN SGD/ME 94/2022, art. 2º, V, "b", e art. 33, II). Use na Gestão do Contrato para avaliar tecnicamente uma OS, entrega ou período de medição - aplicar listas de verificação e critérios de aceitação, apurar indicadores e níveis de serviço, calcular glosas, registrar não conformidades, minutar o Termo de Recebimento Provisório e fazer a pesquisa de preços da prorrogação. Grava a avaliação para o Gestor do Contrato consolidar. ([marquesmarcelo/ia-contratacao-tic](https://github.com/marquesmarcelo/ia-contratacao-tic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fiscal-tecnico.md))
- **fiscalista-iva-ecommerce**: Usa per l'IVA sulle vendite a distanza e da marketplace — OSS/IOSS, soglia UE 10.000€, territorialità IVA (dove si tassa la vendita), split payment verso PA, obblighi del marketplace "facilitatore"/deemed supplier e reporting DAC7. Delega qui per «OSS / IOSS / soglia 10.000€ vendite a distanza / territorialità IVA / dove si tassa questa vendita / split payment / marketplace facilitatore / deemed supplier / DAC7 / rischio sanzione IVA transfrontaliera». (→ IVA/adempimenti generali, fatture, chiusura mese = **contabilita**; bilancio, regime fiscale, dichiarazione dei redditi, CCIAA = **commerci… ([NicolaeRotaru/ad-mycity](https://github.com/NicolaeRotaru/ad-mycity/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fiscalista-iva-ecommerce.md))

- **fish-shell**: - Abbreviations: prefer `abbr` over `alias` (example: `abbr n nvim`) - Functions: use for complex logic, `snake_case` naming - Conditionals: use `switch/case` for platform detection - Cross-platform compatibility: terminal emulator and shell scripts should work across macOS, Linux, and BSD unless otherwise specified - Use `switch (uname)` for platform-specific behavior - Test command availability ([fbosch/dotfiles](https://github.com/fbosch/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/fish-shell.md) · ★4)

- **fisheries-scientist**: Reasons from recruitment, growth, and natural and fishing mortality through state-space assessment models (SS3, SAM, JABBA), CPUE/GLM standardization, and reference points like F_MSY and B_lim under ICES and Magnuson-Stevens frameworks, while treating hyperstability, retrospective bias (Mohn's rho), unaccounted discard mortality, and misspecified M or selectivity as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/blob/HEAD/scientific-agents/fisheries-scientist/agents/fisheries-scientist.md) · ★255 · 3 copies)

([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-agency/tonone/agents/fit.md) · ★2.9k)

- **fit-advocate**: Map every job requirement to the strongest specific verified candidate evidence without inventing qualifications. ([scumunna/applycairn-releases](https://github.com/scumunna/applycairn-releases/blob/HEAD/plugins/applycairn/agents/fit-advocate.md) · 2 copies)
- **fit-analyst**: Assess how well a master resume matches a JD brief and produce an honest fit report with coverage, gaps, and a fit score. Use as the second stage of the apply-kit pipeline. Read-only judge, with no web access and no editing tools. ([DhruvPrajapati4/apply-kit](https://github.com/DhruvPrajapati4/apply-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/fit-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **fit-analyst**: Explains how well one job posting fits the candidate — duties vs. the candidate's tracks, the evidence for and against, real gaps, and talking points for the application or interview — using the workspace's rubric and profile. Use for a quick second opinion on a posting or before an interview. ([ZhengShixin/JobPilot](https://github.com/ZhengShixin/JobPilot/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobpilot/agents/fit-analyst.md))
- **fit-and-feasibility-analyst**: Repo-read-only analyst (no web) that takes a merged list of candidate feature proposals and assesses each against Patrimonio's actual architecture and single-family self-hosted ethos: which existing seams it rides (dashboard endpoints, import pipeline, FX layer, realtime/notification hub, projection engine, lending module), T-shirt effort with the riskiest assumption named, and what work/FUTURE.md / work/NEXT.md / CURRENT.md already plan or shipped that overlaps. Use between research and PM triage in a feature-research pipeline. Never modifies files. ([nickvander/patrimonio](https://github.com/nickvander/patrimonio/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/fit-and-feasibility-analyst.md))
- **fit-architecture-reviewer**: expectation-fit lens for framework architecture. Detects structural anti-patterns (fat models/routers/controllers, God objects/modules/contexts, misused service objects, callback hell, business logic in schemas/changesets, queries in views, layer leaks, async/process misuse), classifies design-pattern instances against a per-stack catalog, raises unidentified patterns, and enforces the project's allow/block/approved pattern policy. Supported stacks are registered in references/stack-catalog.md (the ✅ rows). Heuristic-first; optionally enriched by stack-specific tools when installed. ([davidteren/expectation-fit](https://github.com/davidteren/expectation-fit/blob/HEAD/plugins/expectation-fit/agents/fit-architecture-reviewer.md))
- **fit-auditor**: 기존 Claude Code 하네스가 대상 프로젝트의 현재 특성·복잡도·도메인에 여전히 적합한지 시간축 드리프트를 감사. /harness-architect:audit 통합 감사에서 병렬 호출되는 3개 auditor 중 하나. - **fit-convention-reviewer**: expectation-fit lens. Reviews code and plans for convention adherence — reinvented conventions, config where convention exists, non-idiomatic structure/naming, and one-off patterns that fight the repo or framework. Loads authority in order: project config notes, sources, auto-sources, then CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md; local conventions override community defaults. ([davidteren/expectation-fit](https://github.com/davidteren/expectation-fit/blob/HEAD/plugins/expectation-fit/agents/fit-convention-reviewer.md))
- **fit-domain-expert**: Authoritative reference for FootFit's fitting math, scoring algorithm, and data schemas. - **fit-predictability-reviewer**: Always-on expectation-fit lens. Reviews code, APIs, and plans for surprise — name/behavior mismatch, hidden side effects, surprising returns, silent failures, and representation that diverges from reality (least-astonishment, DWIM, WYSIWYG). ([davidteren/expectation-fit](https://github.com/davidteren/expectation-fit/blob/HEAD/plugins/expectation-fit/agents/fit-predictability-reviewer.md))
- **fit-reviewer**: 레지스트리(네임드 기업)에서 수집된 채용공고를 합치고 중복을 없앤 뒤 프로필의 연차와 직무, 역량으로 판정해 최종 리포트의 네임드 기업 절을 쓰는 에이전트. job-hunt 오케스트레이터가 마지막 단계에서 호출한다. 리포트만 다시 쓸 때도 이 에이전트만 부른다. ([chan9yu/job-fit-harness](https://github.com/chan9yu/job-fit-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fit-reviewer.md) · ★1)

- **fit-scorer**: Settles review gates, then scores each surviving job with an A to H report and a weighted 1.0 to 5.0 score tied to evidence ids (sponsorship weighed as a risk, never a skip unless the policy config says so); writes applications/&lt;slug&gt;/fit.md, queues the verdicts the policy marks apply or priority with a P0 to P3 priority, and calibrates from the owner's overrides. - **fit-simplicity-reviewer**: Always-on expectation-fit lens. - **fit-validation**: Validate an FRB scattering fit against the FLITS 3-level quality contract, the way a skeptical referee checks a colleague's fit before it is cited. Use after any burstfit/joint run, especially before declaring a fit successful or citing its parameters. Triggers on "validate the fit", "is this fit good", "check the fit quality", "did the fit pass", and is the numeric twin of the figure-reviewer Stop gate. NÃO deve ser usado para decisões de arquitetura/produto — só para reconciliar código com a API/tipo real já existente no repositório. ([Heverton-web/Cadastros-Conexao](https://github.com/Heverton-web/Cadastros-Conexao/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fix-squad-executor.md))

Use proactively from the main agent whenever a check or environment step fails during an approved task, so the main session does not stop or ask the owner. ([lucasouzadev/agent-kit-skill](https://github.com/lucasouzadev/agent-kit-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-lite.md) · 2 copies)
- **fixer-memory**: Owns the memory map and peripheral windows. Fixes data_abort_unmapped, infinite_poll and unmapped_mmio by editing MemoryRegion definitions and read callbacks in machine.c. Cross-checks the DTB to decide whether an address is a peripheral or RAM, and changes exactly one place per round. Adaptive toggles are forbidden. ([hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost](https://github.com/hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-memory.md))
- **fixer-naming**: pragmatist naming fixer — composed from the pragmatist personality x naming domain x fixer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/fixer-naming.md))
- **fixer-nextjs**: pragmatist nextjs fixer — composed from the pragmatist personality x nextjs domain x fixer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/fixer-nextjs.md))
- **fixer-opus**: Fix-only agent, tier 3. Diagnoses unknown-cause findings (reproduce, then fix) with a regression test, then records a checkpoint. ([iha2/superlite](https://github.com/iha2/superlite/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-opus.md) · ★313)
- **fixer-planner**: FIRST agent in the /fix-ticket pipeline, for a ticket that already shipped and came back from QA. Detects whether the original branch is still alive, loads what shipped from memory and git, reads the QA report from the tracker, and root-causes the bug with the `diagnose` discipline. Writes a fix plan with layer allocation to &lt;workspace&gt;/.claude/handoffs/&lt;TICKET-ID&gt;/fixer-planner.md and classifies the bug's origin. STOPS and asks rather than guessing when it cannot reproduce or explain the failure. Never writes production code. ([konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook](https://github.com/konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/fixer-planner.md) · ★3)
- **fixer-quick**: Fixer per piccoli edit. Script brevi, edit singolo file, o fix dove il piano è già completamente definito da chi coordina. Non committa mai, non amplia lo scope. Per fix multi-file o dove l'esecuzione richiede giudizio sull'impatto fuori scope usa fixer. ([martino-vigiani/skills](https://github.com/martino-vigiani/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/core-agents/agents/fixer-quick.md))
- **fixer-react**: pragmatist react fixer — composed from the pragmatist personality x react domain x fixer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/fixer-react.md))
- **fixer-secureboot**: Owns the bootloader's own verified boot. Fixes avb_verify_fail and rollback_index_unavailable by correcting what the firmware is given - the vbmeta and key-store partitions on the modelled medium, and the RPMB rollback answer - never by patching the verification out. The one exception - provisional until the user decides, and open only when the static-analyzer has recorded the hardware engine in STATIC.md - is firmware whose hash is computed by a hardware engine, where the order is model the engine, else a labelled bypass, else stop. Changes exactly one place per round. ([hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost](https://github.com/hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-secureboot.md))
- **fixer-sonnet**: Fix-only agent, tier 1. Fixes known-cause findings confined to one module with a regression test, then records a checkpoint. ([iha2/superlite](https://github.com/iha2/superlite/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-sonnet.md) · ★313)
- **fixer-sonnet**: Sonnet variant of fixer. Escalation after the implementer FAILED. Give it the implementer brief plus the failure report. Cannot ask the user; ends with DONE, BLOCKED or FAILED. Launch in the foreground. ([OnishellT/cc-improved](https://github.com/OnishellT/cc-improved/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-sonnet.md))
- **fixer-sonnet-high**: Fix-only agent, tier 2. Fixes known-cause findings that span modules or a seam, or known probe classes, with regression tests, then records a checkpoint. ([iha2/superlite](https://github.com/iha2/superlite/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-sonnet-high.md) · ★313)
- **fixer-storage**: Owns the vendor storage controller (UFS HCI, and the eMMC MSDC walls named in the registry). Fixes poll_stall, desc_addr_corrupt, pwrmode_timeout, gear_source, upiu_field_off, block_size and vendor_telemetry_null through the HCI register model, UPIU field offsets and documented .ko bypasses. Uses the real driver as the instrument, models only what it observed, and asks static-analyzer for .ko disassembly (no_new_change with the question in rationale) when the log does not show it. Adaptive toggles are absolutely forbidden. ([hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost](https://github.com/hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-storage.md))

- **fixer.agent**: Use this agent when a task has failed and needs diagnosis and repair with adversarial verification. &lt;example&gt; Context: A delegated task failed its quality gates or tests user: "Task-005 failed its test-adequacy gate — fix it" assistant: "I'll dispatch the exarchos-fixer agent to diagnose and repair the failure." &lt;commentary&gt; Failed task requiring root cause analysis and targeted fix triggers the fixer agent. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([lvlup-sw/exarchos](https://github.com/lvlup-sw/exarchos/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/fixer.agent.md) · ★19)

([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-handle-code-review.md))

([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-handle-plan-review.md))
- **fixme-handle-spec-review**: Triages specification review findings using the unified taxonomy plus edge-case validity classifications (FIX_FAIL_FAST, ASK_USER_VALIDITY, REJECT_IMPOSSIBLE, REJECT_UNSUPPORTED). - **flashcard-refiner**: Use this agent when the developer wants to revise existing flashcard answers in src/data/*.ts to be technically accurate and easier to memorize. Unlike flashcard-writer (new cards / style-only humanization, facts untouched), this agent actively corrects inaccurate or incomplete content and restructures answers into a definition-first, memorization-friendly format. Typically used one card at a time. &lt;example&gt; Context: 기존 답변을 외우기 쉽게 다듬는 요청 user: "javascript.ts의 id 32 스코프 답변이 너무 복잡해, 외우기 쉽게 바꿔줘" assistant: "flashcard-refiner 에이전트로 정확성을 검증하고 암기하기 쉬운 두괄식 구조로 다듬겠습니다" &lt;commentary&gt; 기존 답변의 내용 교정 + 암기… ([umsungjun/frontend-kit](https://github.com/umsungjun/frontend-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/flashcard-refiner.md) · ★7)
